00123:     w.writerows(summary_rows)
00124: 
00125: print("[GRAPHQL_MASTER_V2] COMPLETE")
00126: print(f"files_scanned={len(files)}")
00127: print(f"rows={len(all_rows)}")
00128: print(f"duplicates_removed={duplicate_count}")
00129: print(f"output={OUT}")
00130: print(f"summary={SUMMARY}")
